# Template changes since the pptxgengo snapshot

Generated by `tools/template_changes.py` from `templates/library` against `pptxgengo-wmds-v1` (commit `d8f45d4`),
the snapshot pinned by pptxgengo `library/wm-design-system/v1`. Machine-readable copy: `templates/changes.json`.

Summary: 552 added, 37 revised, 1 deprecated, 0 removed, 19 moved.

## Frames and renderer

### splits

New split-frame family: the title zone covers a short column only and a tall column runs from y 36 to the body bottom. Four variants: tall-right, tall-left (tall two-thirds, 558 wide) and tall-right-narrow, tall-left-narrow (tall one-third, 270 wide); standard 18 pt gutter (narrowed from a one-column gutter on owner review).

**PowerPoint:** Add four slide layouts (or a split option on the existing no-rail and nav layouts): header placeholders sized to the short column; a 270-wide short column sets the title at heading size (24/30) with up to three lines (rule at 108/126/162), a 558-wide one keeps title size (rule 108/144/180). The header dot patch starts 36 pt left of the short column (not left of x 57 on nav). Stamp and source line follow the short column.

### nav

Nav-rail tabs are now slots: slide.nav = {items: [2-6 section names], active: index}. Previously the labels were hard-coded (Identify, Define, Build, Optimize) with the second always active. Split frames now combine with the nav rail.

**PowerPoint:** Read tab labels and the active tab from slide.nav; tab height = (body bottom - 36 - 6*(n-1)) / n; active tab Grounded with White text, others Light Gray with Dark Gray text. No template used the nav rail before, so this only affects new templates.

### quadrant-markers

Quadrant chart gains key: "markers": numbered filled circles on the points without the built-in key, for templates with their own legend column.

**PowerPoint:** Support key "markers" on the quadrant chart: draw numbered circles at each point and omit the side key.

### table-checkbox

Table cell type checkbox: an empty outlined box for walk-through checklists (true draws a tick). Distinct from check, which reports status (filled tick or dash).

**PowerPoint:** Support cell type checkbox: 12 pt square, 1 pt Grounded outline, no fill; a tick when true. Used by runbook/step-detail-data and runbook/step-detail-gate.

### new-nodes-2026-10-03

Three new node types: logoslot (logo box, contain-fit image or hatched placeholder), dotmap (dot-matrix US map with office and hub markers from lon/lat) and teamcurve (stacked smooth bands across phases from per-point values).

**PowerPoint:** logoslot: picture with contain fit, or a placeholder rectangle with hatch and label. dotmap: either a pre-rendered dot map picture plus native markers and labels, or native 3 pt squares (many shapes; consider one grouped picture). teamcurve: one freeform shape per band built from the same points with Catmull-Rom tension 0.5 converted to cubic Beziers, so editing the values regenerates the curve; phase dividers are lines, labels are text boxes.

### families-2026-10-03

Template families reorganized: openers split into covers (covers, agendas, dividers, closings) and core (key messages, cards, narrative, quotes); architecture moved out of solution; runbooks moved out of approach; about (firm overview, offerings) moved out of proof. Keys (id/variant) are unchanged.

**PowerPoint:** No layout change. Update any family-based grouping or lookup; keys are stable.

### dotmap-americas-uk

dotmap gains region americas-uk: US, Mexico and Central America to Costa Rica plus a framed Great Britain inset (no Ireland, no break mark, after owner review).

**PowerPoint:** Second map panel set; markers land in the panel containing their lon/lat. Inset frame is a 0.75 pt Medium Gray rectangle.

### teamcurve-phaselabels

teamcurve phaseLabels false / phaseH options (used when phase names sit elsewhere).

**PowerPoint:** Honor phaseLabels false: omit the phase label row under the plot.

### dotmap-geography

Dot maps now use real outlines: US Census cartographic shoreline (us-atlas), Natural Earth 1:50m for Mexico, Central America and Great Britain (world-atlas), both public domain, simplified; the Great Lakes are carved out.

**PowerPoint:** If pptxgengo draws the dot field natively, use the same outline data (or a pre-rendered dot picture generated from it) so the maps match.

### slim-footer-tint-title4

Slim footer (rule 495, row 504-516, body to 486); slide tint panels (full-height surface behind the body); titleLines 4 (rule 216).

**PowerPoint:** Add a slim-footer layout variant; tint is a full-height rectangle drawn behind body shapes; support a four-line title zone.

### dots-colored-indicators

Table cell type dots (dot score with optional text below); rating and harvey cells accept per-cell, per-row or per-column ink; status outlines follow the surface ink.

**PowerPoint:** Dots: 8 pt circles, filled in the ink, empty in Medium Gray (Dark Gray on dark). Rating squares and Harvey balls drawn in the given ink.

### venn-legend-dot

New venn node (2-4 circles with set text, overlap regions and plotted points); legend dot swatch with ink.

**PowerPoint:** Venn: native circles with 70% fill transparency and 1 pt Grounded outlines; text boxes at the computed set and overlap anchors; points as small circles with labels.

### venn-placement

Venn overlap labels sit inside their lens; regions take an optional w.

**PowerPoint:** Place pairwise overlap text at the lens point (pair midpoint pushed outward from the diagram centre).

### arrow-straight

New mark arrow-straight: a level hand-drawn arrow pointing right (flipX for left), from graphics/arrows/left-facing-arrow-navy.svg.

**PowerPoint:** Add the straight arrow to the mark library; it places like the other arrow marks (picture, Grounded ink), flipped horizontally for left.

### teamcurve-smoothing

Team curves are smoothed with a monotone cubic (Fritsch–Carlson) by default, so bands round between values without overshooting them. curve: "catmull" keeps the earlier spline.

**PowerPoint:** Build each band edge as a monotone cubic through the values (tangents limited so the curve never overshoots); freeform Bézier segments per interval.

### venn-labels-points

Venn: labelStyle "mono" sets the set labels in mono caps (label style); points are numbered markers (n or order; numbered: false hides) with an optional mono outlined badge to the right or left (side).

**PowerPoint:** Set labels: label style when labelStyle is mono. Points: 18 pt Grounded circle with the number in White mono; badge is a mono 10 pt text box with a 1 pt Grounded outline, 6 pt from the marker on the chosen side.

### table-sub-cells

Table plain cells accept {text, sub}: a second line in small secondary text, e.g. a row label with a subtitle. Both lines use 1.2 line height so two-line cells keep padding in dense rows.

**PowerPoint:** Write the cell as two paragraphs: text at the cell style, sub at 12 pt Dark Gray. Set 1.2 line spacing on both paragraphs.

### maturity

New maturity node: an exponential curve (flat, then steep; shape default 4.2) with numbered 24 pt stage markers, a tick from each marker to its label and text (right-aligned and lifted on the last two steep stages), an active stage, an inflection label, an optional flatter branch from one stage (e.g. 4.5 beyond product delivery) and an axis with a label.

**PowerPoint:** Draw the curve as a freeform through 61 sampled points of f(u)=(e^(s·u)−1)/(e^s−1) with an arrowhead; place stage markers at at[i] (default 0.04+(i/(n−1))^0.8·0.86); text boxes bottom-anchored 4 pt above each tick; branch as a quadratic Bézier to the right edge with its marker at 72%.

### heat

Heat maps: table column type heat shades whole cells on a five-step ramp (seq: #E8EEF8, #B9C9F0, #7C9BFF, #0047FF, #070154 with White text on the top two; risk: #E8EEF8, #CED7E6, #F3D6EE, #FB7FE8, #F900D3 with Grounded text); block heat fills from the same ramp; legend heat swatches.

**PowerPoint:** Heat cell: fill the cell rectangle inset 1.5 pt with the ramp colour for step round(value/max*4); centred bold text (mono for numbers). Block heat: same fill, ink per ramp. Legend: 18x10 swatch.

### families-2026-10-03b

Families reorganized again: status (status, steering, RAID) moved out of evidence into status; venn moved out of evidence into venn; team-curve moved out of approach into team-curves. New families: heatmaps, software, modernization, maturity. Keys (id/variant) are unchanged.

**PowerPoint:** No layout change. Update any family-based grouping or lookup; keys are stable.

### maturity-here-headroom

maturity node: here {stage, label} draws a Magenta mono tag under a marker joined by a 12 pt Magenta tick; headroom (pt) lowers the top of the curve area so the top stage text clears the title rule.

**PowerPoint:** here: 1.5 pt Magenta line 12 pt below the marker, then a text box with Magenta fill, Grounded mono 10 pt bold text, centred on the marker. headroom: offset the curve's top by headroom; the axis stays at the bottom.

### venn-r12

Venn labels are always mono caps: set labels 13 pt, pairwise overlap labels 11 pt, the all-sets centre 13 pt (labelStyle "subhead" keeps the sans set label). Overlap anchors are computed as the middle of the stretch, walking outward from the centroid, that lies inside the listed circles and outside the others. Labels accept dx/dy nudges. Point numbers 10 pt, badges 9.5 pt.

**PowerPoint:** Set label text boxes in IBM Plex Mono 600 caps with 0.06 em tracking at 13 pt (11 pt for pairwise overlaps). Compute overlap anchors with the same sampling (step 0.01 R from −1 R to 2 R along the centroid's outward direction). Apply dx/dy offsets.

### callout-surface

callout node gains surface: "callout" (Magenta, default) or "inverse" (Grounded with White text).

**PowerPoint:** Fill and inks from the chosen surface's role set.

### maturity-six

maturity node supports 3–6 stages; with 6 stages the last three are right-aligned and lifted (last two below 6).

**PowerPoint:** Right-align and lift the text of the last three stages when there are six.

### funnel-pyramid-road-cycle-bracket

New nodes: funnel (trapezoid bands narrowing to a neck, darkest at the bottom, optional side text column with dotted leaders), pyramid (levels widening from an apex, darkest at top), road (winding road band with dashed centre line and numbered milestone pins, labels alternating above/below, mirrored with direction left), cycle (items around an ellipse joined clockwise by arrowed arcs, active item on Grounded, centre block, dashed Blue loop-back arrows), bracket (square bracket with centre tick and label).

**PowerPoint:** funnel/pyramid: one freeform trapezoid per band, fill ramp #E8EEF8 #CED7E6 #B9C9F0 #7C9BFF #0047FF #070154 (White text on the two darkest); side text boxes with a 1 pt dotted #97A4BA connector. road: a freeform polyline of the sampled path y = top + h(0.82 − 0.64u) + h·amp·sin(2π·waves·u) at 22 pt Grounded, overlaid by a 1.5 pt White dashed line; 26 pt pins. cycle: rounded-free rectangles on an ellipse (rx = (w − nodeW)/2, ry = (h − nodeH)/2), arcs as freeform polylines with open arrowheads, loops as dashed quadratic curves. bracket: three-segment line plus tick.

### teamcurve-gaussian

teamcurve now eases by default: each cumulative band edge is linearly resampled at 241 points and Gaussian-blurred with sigma = smooth × width (default 0.07), which rounds plateau corners and spreads steep ramps; smooth: 0 restores the monotone cubic.

**PowerPoint:** Compute the same 241-point smoothed edges (linear interpolation of the cumulative values, then Gaussian blur with edge clamping) and draw each band as one freeform polyline (top edge forward, lower edge back). Editing values regenerates the shape.

### status-states

Status values gain work-item states: done (Grounded), progress (Blue), notstarted (White), blocked (Red), pass (Green), fail (Red), alongside on/risk/off. Status legends list on/risk/off unless statuses is set.

**PowerPoint:** Extend the status map: same 8 pt square with a 0.75 pt Grounded outline, fills as listed, labels Done / In progress / Not started / Blocked / Pass / Fail.

### status-labels

Status labels can be renamed without changing the colour: table status columns take `labels: {status: text}`, a status cell may be `{status, label}`. Legend items take `status` (8 pt status square; text defaults to the status name).

**PowerPoint:** Status cell text: cell label, then column `labels[status]`, then the default status name; colour unchanged. Legend item with `status`: draw the status square (8 pt, outlined) and its text.

### source-wrap

A source line wider than its column (measured at 9 pt sans) wraps onto two lines and the body bottom moves up to 432. Applies mostly to the 270 pt short column of tall-right / tall-left split frames. Full 'Sample figures… replace with current firm data before use.' wording restored on value-bridge/levers-split, value-curve/break-even-split, case-study/exhibit-split and understanding-objectives/measures-split.

**PowerPoint:** Source text box: if the text does not fit the column on one line, allow two lines (12 pt leading) anchored to the same bottom, rather than truncating.

### families-2026-10-03-r14

Three new families: understanding (Our understanding, 10), interviews (Stakeholder interviews, 24), offers (One-page proposals, 15). Gallery groups: understanding under Openers, interviews under Delivery, offers under Commercial and firm.

**PowerPoint:** New templates only; they compose existing nodes (table with status labels and {text,sub} cells, gantt, cards, callouts, legend status items, metrics, thumbnails). Implement the status-labels and source-wrap capabilities first.

### table-row-height-ref

Tables: a row may set `h` (its own height in pt; the table is the sum of row heights), for notes rows whose `bullets` column holds 2-4 bullets. A plain `{text, sub?, ref}` cell draws a mono reference badge (e.g. C3) before the text; `refActive: true` fills it Grounded with White text. Used to tie a pure heat map's rows to notes slides. Correction 2026-10-05: row.h is a row height only when it is a number and no column is keyed "h" (several tables use a column keyed h; their cell values must not be read as heights).

**PowerPoint:** Row heights: use row.h when present instead of rowH for that row (cell boxes, rules, heat tiles). Reference badge: a small text box before the cell text, IBM Plex Mono 9 pt semibold, 0.75 pt Grounded outline, 3 pt side padding; filled Grounded with White text when refActive. Only treat row.h as a height when it is numeric and no column has k == "h".

### heat-min

Heat scales take a minimum: table heat columns `min` (or table `heatMin`), blocks `heatMin`, legend items `heatMin`. The five steps span min..max (default 0..4), so a 1-5 score is stored as itself with min 1, max 5. The new heat-notes/* and heat-ref/* templates use it.

**PowerPoint:** heatStep = round((value - min) / (max - min) * 4), clamped to 0..4; min defaults to 0 and max to 4. Apply to table heat cells, heat blocks and heat legend swatches.

### table-rowgroups-priority

Tables: `rowGroups: [{label, from, to, fill?}]` draws vertical group cells down the far left (as on the Gantt), spanning body rows from..to; `groupW` (default 24), columns shift right by groupW + 6. New column type `priority`: centered mono chip, P1-P4 (P1 Grounded fill / White, P2 #32477B / White, P3 #CED7E6 / Grounded, P4 outline) or now / next / later (Grounded fill, #CED7E6, outline).

**PowerPoint:** rowGroups: a filled rectangle per group from the first to the last spanned row (1.5 pt inset top and bottom), text rotated 270 degrees, IBM Plex Mono 8 pt semibold caps, White on dark fills else Grounded; table columns start at x + groupW + 6. Priority: a small text box with fill and 0.75 pt Grounded outline, IBM Plex Mono 9 pt semibold caps, min width 40 pt, centered in the cell.

### families-2026-10-05

New family: workshops (Workshops, 14 templates): workshop overviews (pre-workshop one-pagers with agenda, focus areas, outcomes, attendees, pre-work), workshop series, workshop readouts (learnings, decisions, outcomes against objectives, next steps, prioritization) and facilitation run-of-show plans. Gallery group: Delivery, after Stakeholder interviews.

**PowerPoint:** New templates only; they compose existing nodes (bullets, ol, vstepper, table with status labels, priority chips, dots and rowGroups, pullquote, nav rail, inverse panel).

### frames-docs-2026-10-05

frames/v0/frames.json gained descriptive text, grouped board examples and two documentation-only geometry tables (title-zone rules for 3 and 4 title lines, and a body-bottom table for compact/tall/slim footers with 0/1/2 source lines). The renderer's constants are unchanged.

**PowerPoint:** No change needed: values match the renderer and authoring-reference (titleLines 3 → rule 180, 4 → rule 216; body bottoms compact 468/450/432, tall 450/432, slim 486/468/450).

### roadfork

New node `roadfork`: a road that forks. Trunk milestones (foundation) run to a fork, then 2-3 branch roads curve into lanes with their own milestones and titles. mode parallel (split marker, solid roads, 'Run in parallel') or decision (Magenta '?' diamond, 'Decision', dashed option roads; chosen draws one solid and greys the rest). Also: fork.tag overrides the fork eyebrow; with chosen set the diamond shows ✓ and the eyebrow reads 'Decided'; milestones may set here: true (hereLabel) for a Magenta pin with a 'You are here' tag. Branch title row: the Track/Option tag and the title sit on one line. Three branches default to 12 pt milestone labels (small).

**PowerPoint:** Draw roads as thick rounded Grounded polylines/beziers (stroke ~18 pt) with a 1.5 pt White dashed centre line (7/7; 3/5 for unchosen options). Trunk from x+14 to fork x (w*at); each branch: cubic from fork point to lane y over ~22% of remaining width (max 108 pt), then straight to x+w-14. Lane i centre y = y + h/n*i + h/n*0.42. Pins: 24 pt circles, White fill, 1.5 pt ring, mono 10 pt labels (1..n on trunk, A1.. on branches); greyed options use #97A4BA ring and text, road #CED7E6. Fork: decision = 26 pt square rotated 45 deg, Magenta fill, '?'; parallel = 22 pt Grounded square with White inner ring. Labels: trunk alternate above/below; branch milestone labels below pins; branch eyebrow 'Track/Option A' plus subhead title above the lane start. here: Magenta pin fill plus a small Grounded label box (mono 9 pt White caps, 'You are here') centred 22 pt above the pin.

### family-roadmaps-2026-10-06

New family roadmaps (Roadmaps). Moved without content changes: diagrams road/* and timeline/* (diagrams is renamed 'Funnels and pyramids'), software roadmap/* (now/next/later, structures, dense grids, confidence and status), approach roadmap/staggered-phases(-nav). Approach section 'Plans and roadmaps' renamed 'Plans and cutover'.

**PowerPoint:** Template keys are unchanged; only family and gallery section change. Update any family-based lookups.

### typography-density

Typography density, three levels: comfortable (today's standard, default), compact, dense. Slide field density sets the body; headerDensity sets eyebrow and title. Values per role are in tokens/v0/tokens.json → density (header: title, heading, eyebrow; body: heading, subhead, lead, body, small, label, eyebrow, number, stat, stat-sm, cell-body, cell-small, list gaps). Only type size, leading and bullet spacing change; box geometry, padding, table row heights and title-zone rules do not. No per-component density. Floor 8 pt (source and legal unchanged). 'standard' is an alias of comfortable; 'appendix' keeps its older meaning. Bullet placement is part of density too: list indent 15/12/11 (small lists 12/11/10), marker 4/3/3 (small 3/3/2.5), gap 6/3/2 (small 3/2/1), numbered-list number column 18/15/14 (small 15/14/13); compact's normal list therefore matches the old small list exactly.

**PowerPoint:** Resolve each text run's style through tokens.density[level][role] instead of the fixed type scale: header runs (eyebrow, slide title) use headerDensity, all other runs the slide's density; table cell text uses cell-body / cell-small (and anything nested in a cell follows the cell scale). Keep shape geometry identical. For dynamic fit: try the authored level, then step down one level at a time (comfortable → compact → dense), never below dense and never continuous scaling; if dense still overflows, flag the slide for a split. Header density only changes if a title needs a third line on a template that allows it. Bullets: use tokens.density list-indent / list-marker / list-gap (and the -small variants) for indent, square marker size and paragraph spacing; numbered lists use ol-col for the number column.

### density-migration-2026-10-06

Density migration: all 215 templates that carried component-level small overrides (659 overrides: bullets/ol size, card bodySize, strongnum size) now set one slide density instead; all chose compact. No component-level small overrides remain in the library. Families: about 1, approach 8, architecture 7, argument 26, change 9, commercials 4, core 5, covers 1, decisions 7, diagrams 2, evidence 1, heatmaps 22, interviews 12, lifecycle 5, maturity 6, modernization 7, offers 12, proof 1, roadmaps 11, runbooks 11, software 2, solution 9, status 1, team 3, team-curves 3, understanding 9, value 6, venn 3, workshops 21. Per-template notes list the exact nodes.

**PowerPoint:** For each listed template: set slide density compact and stop applying per-component small sizes; resolve every run through tokens.density compact (body 12/17, small 11/15, subhead 16/21, label 8/11, cell-body 12/17, cell-small 10/13). Former-small nodes keep 12 pt; the rest of the slide steps down to match. Geometry unchanged. Templates with density appendix were not changed.

Changed keys in `frames/v0/frames.json`: chrome, examples, features, footers, rails, splits, summary, zones.

  - Four new curves (rev 1, added 2026-10-03).
